Our approach consistently improves state-of-the-art foundation models, including Depth Anything 3 and VGGT, across 4 benchmark datasets, yielding an average improvement of 3.73% in camera pose accuracy and 2.88% in point map prediction. Code is available at https://github.com/hiteacherIamhumble/Free-Geometry .

</details>


### [75] [OneHOI: Unifying Human-Object Interaction Generation and Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14062)
*Jiun Tian Hoe,Weipeng Hu,Xudong Jiang,Yap-Peng Tan,Chee Seng Chan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OneHOI：统一的扩散Transformer框架，将HOI生成和编辑整合到单一条件去噪过程中，通过结构化交互表示实现混合条件控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有HOI方法存在局限性：HOI生成方法只能从结构化三元组和布局合成场景，无法整合混合条件（如HOI和仅对象实体）；HOI编辑方法通过文本修改交互，但难以解耦姿势与物理接触，且难以扩展到多交互场景。

Method: 提出OneHOI统一框架，核心是关系扩散Transformer（R-DiT），通过角色和实例感知的HOI令牌、基于布局的空间动作定位、结构化HOI注意力来强制交互拓扑、以及HOI RoPE来解耦多HOI场景。采用模态丢弃联合训练，支持布局引导、无布局、任意掩码和混合条件控制。

Result: 在HOI生成和编辑任务上均取得最先进结果，支持多种控制模式，包括布局引导、布局自由、任意掩码和混合条件控制。

Conclusion: OneHOI成功统一了HOI生成和编辑任务，通过结构化交互表示和创新的扩散Transformer架构，解决了现有方法的局限性，为复杂人-物交互建模提供了更灵活强大的框架。

Abstract: Human-Object Interaction (HOI) modelling captures how humans act upon and relate to objects, typically expressed as <person, action, object> triplets. Existing approaches split into two disjoint families: HOI generation synthesises scenes from structured triplets and layout, but fails to integrate mixed conditions like HOI and object-only entities; and HOI editing modifies interactions via text, yet struggles to decouple pose from physical contact and scale to multiple interactions. We introduce OneHOI, a unified diffusion transformer framework that consolidates HOI generation and editing into a single conditional denoising process driven by shared structured interaction representations. At its core, the Relational Diffusion Transformer (R-DiT) models verb-mediated relations through role- and instance-aware HOI tokens, layout-based spatial Action Grounding, a Structured HOI Attention to enforce interaction topology, and HOI RoPE to disentangle multi-HOI scenes. Trained jointly with modality dropout on our HOI-Edit-44K, along with HOI and object-centric datasets, OneHOI supports layout-guided, layout-free, arbitrary-mask, and mixed-condition control, achieving state-of-the-art results across both HOI generation and editing. Code is available at https://jiuntian.github.io/OneHOI/.

</details>


### [76] [Towards Unconstrained Human-Object Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14069)
*Francesco Tonini,Alessandro Conti,Lorenzo Vaquero,Cigdem Beyan,Elisa Ricci*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出无约束人-物交互检测任务，利用多模态大语言模型在开放环境中识别任意交互，摆脱了传统方法对预定义交互词汇表的依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统人-物交互检测方法依赖预定义的交互词汇表，限制了其在动态开放环境中的应用。随着多模态大语言模型的发展，有必要探索更灵活的交互识别范式。

Method: 定义无约束人-物交互任务，评估多种MLLM模型，提出包含测试时推理和语言到图转换的流程，从自由文本中提取结构化交互信息。

Result: 研究揭示了当前HOI检测器的局限性，并证明了MLLM在无约束人-物交互任务中的价值，为开放环境下的交互识别提供了新思路。

Conclusion: 通过多模态大语言模型重新审视人-物交互检测，提出的无约束人-物交互任务为更灵活、适应性更强的交互识别系统奠定了基础。

Abstract: Human-Object Interaction (HOI) detection is a longstanding computer vision problem concerned with predicting the interaction between humans and objects. Current HOI models rely on a vocabulary of interactions at training and inference time, limiting their applicability to static environments. With the advent of Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs), it has become feasible to explore more flexible paradigms for interaction recognition. In this work, we revisit HOI detection through the lens of MLLMs and apply them to in-the-wild HOI detection. We define the Unconstrained HOI (U-HOI) task, a novel HOI domain that removes the requirement for a predefined list of interactions at both training and inference. We evaluate a range of MLLMs on this setting and introduce a pipeline that includes test-time inference and language-to-graph conversion to extract structured interactions from free-form text. Our findings highlight the limitations of current HOI detectors and the value of MLLMs for U-HOI. Code will be available at https://github.com/francescotonini/anyhoi

</details>


### [77] [Don't Let the Video Speak: Audio-Contrastive Preference Optimization for Audio-Visual Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14129)
*Ami Baid,Zihui Xue,Kristen Grauman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ACPO通过双轴偏好学习框架解决AVLM中的跨模态幻觉问题，特别针对视频驱动的音频幻觉，通过输出对比和输入对比目标惩罚视觉主导的音频描述


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 音频-视觉语言模型存在跨模态幻觉瓶颈，特别是视频驱动的音频幻觉问题：模型过度依赖视觉线索来"幻觉"预期声音，而忽略真实的听觉证据

Method: 提出音频对比偏好优化（ACPO），包含输出对比目标（惩罚伪装成音频事实的视觉描述）和输入对比目标（通过交换音轨惩罚对真实听觉信号不变性的生成）

Result: 广泛实验表明ACPO建立了高度忠实的音频基础，缓解了音频幻觉问题，同时不损害整体多模态能力

Conclusion: ACPO通过双轴偏好学习有效对抗视觉主导，提高了音频-视觉语言模型的可靠性和音频基础能力

Abstract: While Audio-Visual Language Models (AVLMs) have achieved remarkable progress over recent years, their reliability is bottlenecked by cross-modal hallucination. A particularly pervasive manifestation is video-driven audio hallucination: models routinely exploit visual shortcuts to hallucinate expected sounds, discarding true auditory evidence. To counteract this deeply ingrained visual dominance, we propose Audio-Contrastive Preference Optimization (ACPO). This dual-axis preference learning framework introduces an output-contrastive objective to penalize visual descriptions masquerading as audio facts, alongside an input-contrastive objective that swaps audio tracks to explicitly penalize generation invariant to the true auditory signal. Extensive experiments demonstrate that ACPO establishes highly faithful audio grounding and mitigates audio hallucination without compromising overarching multimodal capabilities.

</details>


### [78] [Geometric Context Transformer for Streaming 3D Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14141)
*Lin-Zhuo Chen,Jian Gao,Yihang Chen,Ka Leong Cheng,Yipengjing Sun,Liangxiao Hu,Nan Xue,Xing Zhu,Yujun Shen,Yao Yao,Yinghao Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LingBot-Map：基于几何上下文Transformer的流式3D重建基础模型，通过创新的注意力机制实现几何精度、时间一致性和计算效率，在长序列上达到约20FPS的实时性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式3D重建需要从视频流中恢复3D信息（相机姿态和点云），这要求几何精度、时间一致性和计算效率。受SLAM原理启发，需要开发一个能够处理长序列、保持几何上下文并实时运行的3D基础模型。

Method: 提出LingBot-Map，基于几何上下文Transformer架构的流式3D重建基础模型。核心是精心设计的注意力机制，包含三个关键组件：1）锚点上下文用于坐标接地；2）姿态参考窗口提供密集几何线索；3）轨迹记忆用于长距离漂移校正。这种设计保持流式状态紧凑同时保留丰富几何上下文。

Result: 在518×378分辨率输入上，能够在超过10,000帧的长序列上实现约20FPS的稳定高效推理。在多个基准测试上的广泛评估表明，该方法在性能上优于现有的流式方法和迭代优化方法。

Conclusion: LingBot-Map通过创新的注意力机制设计，成功解决了流式3D重建中的坐标接地、几何线索提取和长距离漂移校正等关键问题，实现了高效、准确的实时3D场景重建，为流式3D重建提供了新的基础模型解决方案。

Abstract: Streaming 3D reconstruction aims to recover 3D information, such as camera poses and point clouds, from a video stream, which necessitates geometric accuracy, temporal
  consistency, and computational efficiency. Motivated by the principles of Simultaneous Localization and Mapping (SLAM), we introduce LingBot-Map, a feed-forward 3D foundation
  model for reconstructing scenes from streaming data, built upon a geometric context transformer (GCT) architecture. A defining aspect of LingBot-Map lies in its carefully
  designed attention mechanism, which integrates an anchor context, a pose-reference window, and a trajectory memory to address coordinate grounding, dense geometric cues, and
  long-range drift correction, respectively. This design keeps the streaming state compact while retaining rich geometric context, enabling stable efficient inference at around
  20 FPS on 518 x 378 resolution inputs over long sequences exceeding 10,000 frames. Extensive evaluations across a variety of benchmarks demonstrate that our approach
  achieves superior performance compared to both existing streaming and iterative optimization-based approaches.

</details>


### [79] [SpatialEvo: Self-Evolving Spatial Intelligence via Deterministic Geometric Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14144)
*Dinging Li,Yingxiu Zhao,Xinrui Cheng,Kangheng Lin,Hongbo Peng,Hongxing Li,Zixuan Wang,Yuhong Dai,Haodong Li,Jia Wang,Yukang Shi,Liang Zhao,Jianjian Sun,Zheng Ge,Xiangyu Zhang,Weiming Lu,Jun Xiao,Yueting Zhuang,Yongliang Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SpatialEvo：基于确定性几何环境的3D空间推理自演化框架，通过几何验证规则而非模型共识生成无噪声伪标签，实现高效训练


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三维场景空间推理是具身智能的核心能力，但连续模型改进受限于几何标注成本。现有自演化范式依赖模型共识构建伪标签，导致训练会强化而非纠正模型的几何错误

Method: 提出SpatialEvo框架，核心是确定性几何环境（DGE），将16个空间推理任务类别形式化为显式几何验证规则，将未标注3D场景转换为零噪声交互式oracle。单一共享参数策略在提问者和解答者角色下协同演化：提问者基于场景观察生成物理有效的空间问题，解答者针对DGE验证的真实答案推导精确答案。任务自适应调度器内生地集中训练于模型最弱类别

Result: 在9个基准测试中，SpatialEvo在3B和7B规模上均获得最高平均分，在空间推理基准上取得一致增益，在通用视觉理解上没有性能下降

Conclusion: 通过利用3D空间推理的独特属性——真实答案是底层几何的确定性结果，可以从点云和相机姿态精确计算而不依赖模型，SpatialEvo克服了自演化范式中的伪标签噪声问题，实现了高效的三维空间推理能力提升

Abstract: Spatial reasoning over three-dimensional scenes is a core capability for embodied intelligence, yet continuous model improvement remains bottlenecked by the cost of geometric annotation. The self-evolving paradigm offers a promising path, but its reliance on model consensus to construct pseudo-labels causes training to reinforce rather than correct the model's own geometric errors. We identify a property unique to 3D spatial reasoning that circumvents this limitation: ground truth is a deterministic consequence of the underlying geometry, computable exactly from point clouds and camera poses without any model involvement. Building on this insight, we present SpatialEvo, a self-evolving framework for 3D spatial reasoning, centered on the Deterministic Geometric Environment (DGE). The DGE formalizes 16 spatial reasoning task categories under explicit geometric validation rules and converts unannotated 3D scenes into zero-noise interactive oracles, replacing model consensus with objective physical feedback. A single shared-parameter policy co-evolves across questioner and solver roles under DGE constraints: the questioner generates physically valid spatial questions grounded in scene observations, while the solver derives precise answers against DGE-verified ground truth. A task-adaptive scheduler endogenously concentrates training on the model's weakest categories, producing a dynamic curriculum without manual design. Experiments across nine benchmarks demonstrate that SpatialEvo achieves the highest average score at both 3B and 7B scales, with consistent gains on spatial reasoning benchmarks and no degradation on general visual understanding.

</details>


### [80] [Seedance 2.0: Advancing Video Generation for World Complexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14148)
*Team Seedance,De Chen,Liyang Chen,Xin Chen,Ying Chen,Zhuo Chen,Zhuowei Chen,Feng Cheng,Tianheng Cheng,Yufeng Cheng,Mojie Chi,Xuyan Chi,Jian Cong,Qinpeng Cui,Fei Ding,Qide Dong,Yujiao Du,Haojie Duanmu,Junliang Fan,Jiarui Fang,Jing Fang,Zetao Fang,Chengjian Feng,Yu Gao,Diandian Gu,Dong Guo,Hanzhong Guo,Qiushan Guo,Boyang Hao,Hongxiang Hao,Haoxun He,Jiaao He,Qian He,Tuyen Hoang,Heng Hu,Ruoqing Hu,Yuxiang Hu,Jiancheng Huang,Weilin Huang,Zhaoyang Huang,Zhongyi Huang,Jishuo Jin,Ming Jing,Ashley Kim,Shanshan Lao,Yichong Leng,Bingchuan Li,Gen Li,Haifeng Li,Huixia Li,Jiashi Li,Ming Li,Xiaojie Li,Xingxing Li,Yameng Li,Yiying Li,Yu Li,Yueyan Li,Chao Liang,Han Liang,Jianzhong Liang,Ying Liang,Wang Liao,J. H. Lien,Shanchuan Lin,Xi Lin,Feng Ling,Yue Ling,Fangfang Liu,Jiawei Liu,Jihao Liu,Jingtuo Liu,Shu Liu,Sichao Liu,Wei Liu,Xue Liu,Zuxi Liu,Ruijie Lu,Lecheng Lyu,Jingting Ma,Tianxiang Ma,Xiaonan Nie,Jingzhe Ning,Junjie Pan,Xitong Pan,Ronggui Peng,Xueqiong Qu,Yuxi Ren,Yuchen Shen,Guang Shi,Lei Shi,Yinglong Song,Fan Sun,Li Sun,Renfei Sun,Wenjing Tang,Boyang Tao,Zirui Tao,Dongliang Wang,Feng Wang,Hulin Wang,Ke Wang,Qingyi Wang,Rui Wang,Shuai Wang,Shulei Wang,Weichen Wang,Xuanda Wang,Yanhui Wang,Yue Wang,Yuping Wang,Yuxuan Wang,Zijie Wang,Ziyu Wang,Guoqiang Wei,Meng Wei,Di Wu,Guohong Wu,Hanjie Wu,Huachao Wu,Jian Wu,Jie Wu,Ruolan Wu,Shaojin Wu,Xiaohu Wu,Xinglong Wu,Yonghui Wu,Ruiqi Xia,Xin Xia,Xuefeng Xiao,Shuang Xu,Bangbang Yang,Jiaqi Yang,Runkai Yang,Tao Yang,Yihang Yang,Zhixian Yang,Ziyan Yang,Fulong Ye,Bingqian Yi,Xing Yin,Yongbin You,Linxiao Yuan,Weihong Zeng,Xuejiao Zeng,Yan Zeng,Siyu Zhai,Zhonghua Zhai,Bowen Zhang,Chenlin Zhang,Heng Zhang,Jun Zhang,Manlin Zhang,Peiyuan Zhang,Shuo Zhang,Xiaohe Zhang,Xiaoying Zhang,Xinyan Zhang,Xinyi Zhang,Yichi Zhang,Zixiang Zhang,Haiyu Zhao,Huating Zhao,Liming Zhao,Yian Zhao,Guangcong Zheng,Jianbin Zheng,Xiaozheng Zheng,Zerong Zheng,Kuan Zhu,Feilong Zuo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Seedance 2.0是中国2026年初发布的新型原生多模态音视频生成模型，采用统一高效的大规模架构，支持文本、图像、音频、视频四种输入模态，提供行业最全面的多模态内容参考和编辑能力，在音视频生成各维度均有显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发更先进的多模态音视频生成模型，超越前代Seedance 1.0和1.5 Pro，提供更全面的多模态输入支持和更强大的生成能力，满足用户对高质量音视频内容创作的需求。

Method: 采用统一、高效、大规模的多模态音视频联合生成架构，支持四种输入模态（文本、图像、音频、视频），集成行业最全面的多模态内容参考和编辑套件，提供加速版本Seedance 2.0 Fast用于低延迟场景。

Result: 模型在专家评估和公开用户测试中达到领域领先水平，支持4-15秒音视频内容直接生成，原生输出分辨率480p和720p，多模态输入支持最多3个视频片段、9张图像、3个音频片段，基础生成能力和多模态生成性能均有显著改进。

Conclusion: Seedance 2.0在音视频生成领域实现了重大技术进步，提供了增强的创作体验，其统一架构和全面功能使其成为行业领先的多模态音视频生成解决方案。

Abstract: Seedance 2.0 is a new native multi-modal audio-video generation model, officially released in China in early February 2026. Compared with its predecessors, Seedance 1.0 and 1.5 Pro, Seedance 2.0 adopts a unified, highly efficient, and large-scale architecture for multi-modal audio-video joint generation. This allows it to support four input modalities: text, image, audio, and video, by integrating one of the most comprehensive suites of multi-modal content reference and editing capabilities available in the industry to date. It delivers substantial, well-rounded improvements across all key sub-dimensions of video and audio generation. In both expert evaluations and public user tests, the model has demonstrated performance on par with the leading levels in the field. Seedance 2.0 supports direct generation of audio-video content with durations ranging from 4 to 15 seconds, with native output resolutions of 480p and 720p. For multi-modal inputs as reference, its current open platform supports up to 3 video clips, 9 images, and 3 audio clips. In addition, we provide Seedance 2.0 Fast version, an accelerated variant of Seedance 2.0 designed to boost generation speed for low-latency scenarios. Seedance 2.0 has delivered significant improvements to its foundational generation capabilities and multi-modal generation performance, bringing an enhanced creative experience for end users.

</details>


### [81] [One Token per Highly Selective Frame: Towards Extreme Compression for Long Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14149)
*Zheyu Zhang,Ziqi Pang,Shixing Chen,Xiang Hao,Vimal Bhat,Yu-Xiong Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出LP-Comp和QC-Comp两种压缩方法，实现长视频理解中每帧仅需一个token的极端压缩，提升模型处理帧数2-4倍


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视频理解面临帧数过多导致token爆炸的问题，现有基于启发式压缩的方法容易丢失信息，需要更有效的压缩策略来保留时序信息

Method: 提出LP-Comp（可学习渐进式token级压缩）和QC-Comp（问题条件化帧级压缩），结合局部注意力缓解位置偏差，实现token和帧的双重压缩

Result: 在LVBench上准确率从42.9%提升至46.2%，仅需2.5%的监督微调数据，处理帧数增加2-4倍，在多个长视频基准上表现提升

Conclusion: 通过token级和帧级的双重压缩策略，实现了长视频理解的高效极端压缩，显著提升了模型处理长视频的能力和性能

Abstract: Long video understanding is inherently challenging for vision-language models (VLMs) because of the extensive number of frames. With each video frame typically expanding into tens or hundreds of tokens, the limited context length of large language models (LLMs) forces the VLMs to perceive the frames sparsely and lose temporal information. To address this, we explore extreme video token compression towards \emph{one token per frame} at the final LLM layer. Our key insight is that heuristic-based compression, widely adopted by previous methods, is prone to information loss, and this necessitates supervising LLM layers into \emph{learnable} and \emph{progressive} modules for \emph{token-level compression} (LP-Comp). Such compression enables our VLM to digest 2x-4x more frames with improved performance. To further increase the token efficiency, we investigate \emph{frame-level compression}, which selects the frames most relevant to the queries via the internal attention scores of the LLM layers, named \emph{question-conditioned compression} (QC-Comp). As a notable distinction from previous studies, we mitigate the position bias of LLM attention in long contexts, \emph{i.e.}, the over-concentration on the beginning and end of a sequence, by splitting long videos into short segments and employing local attention. Collectively, our combined \emph{token-level} and \emph{frame-level} leads to an e\textbf{x}treme compression model for long video understanding, named \textbf{\name}, achieving a significantly larger compression ratio and enabling denser frame sampling. Our \name is finetuned from VideoChat-Flash with a data-efficient \emph{supervised compression tuning} stage that only requires 2.5\% of the supervised fine-tuning data, yet boosts the accuracy from 42.9\% to 46.2\% on LVBench and enhances multiple other long video benchmarks.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

### [82] [Multi-modal panoramic 3D outdoor datasets for place categorization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13142)
*Hojung Jung,Yuki Oto,Oscar M. Mozos,Yumi Iwashita,Ryo Kurazume*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了两个用于语义场所分类的多模态全景3D室外数据集，包含森林、海岸、住宅区、城区和室内/外停车场六个类别，分别包含650个密集点云扫描和34,200个稀疏点云扫描，并在福冈市采集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为语义场所分类任务提供高质量的多模态全景3D室外数据集，解决现有数据集中缺乏全景3D数据的问题，支持密集和稀疏点云两种场景的研究。

Method: 使用FARO激光扫描仪获取650个密集彩色反射点云（900万点）和同步彩色图像；使用Velodyne激光扫描仪在车辆行驶中获取34,200个稀疏反射点云（7万点）。数据集在福冈市采集并公开。

Result: 创建了两个公开可用的MPO数据集：密集数据集（650个扫描）和稀疏数据集（34,200个扫描）。语义场所分类的最佳准确率达到96.42%（密集）和89.67%（稀疏）。

Conclusion: 提出的多模态全景3D室外数据集为语义场所分类提供了有价值的资源，实验结果表明在密集和稀疏点云上都能获得高分类准确率，验证了数据集的实用性和有效性。

Abstract: We present two multi-modal panoramic 3D outdoor (MPO) datasets for semantic place categorization with six categories: forest, coast, residential area, urban area and indoor/outdoor parking lot. The first dataset consists of 650 static panoramic scans of dense (9,000,000 points) 3D color and reflectance point clouds obtained using a FARO laser scanner with synchronized color images. The second dataset consists of 34,200 real-time panoramic scans of sparse (70,000 points) 3D reflectance point clouds obtained using a Velodyne laser scanner while driving a car. The datasets were obtained in the city of Fukuoka, Japan and are publicly available in [1], [2]. In addition, we compare several approaches for semantic place categorization with best results of 96.42% (dense) and 89.67% (sparse).

</details>


### [83] [Weakly-supervised Learning for Physics-informed Neural Motion Planning via Sparse Roadmap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13204)
*Ruiqi Ni,Yuchen Liu,Ahmed H. Qureshi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: H-NTFields：一种结合稀疏路图弱监督与物理信息PDE正则化的分层神经时间场框架，用于解决复杂多房间环境中的运动规划问题，相比现有物理信息方法显著提升了鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于物理信息的方法（如NTFields）在复杂多房间环境中难以扩展，仅增加采样数量无法解决局部极小值或保证全局一致性，需要结合全局拓扑信息来改进。

Method: 提出分层神经时间场（H-NTFields）框架，将稀疏路图的弱监督（提供行程时间的上下界作为全局拓扑锚点）与物理信息PDE正则化（强制局部几何保真度和障碍物感知传播）相结合。

Result: 在18个Gibson环境和真实机器人平台上实验表明，H-NTFields相比先前物理信息方法显著提高了鲁棒性，同时通过连续值表示实现了快速摊销推理。

Conclusion: H-NTFields通过结合全局拓扑锚点和局部物理约束，有效解决了复杂多房间环境中的运动规划问题，为物理信息方法在复杂场景中的应用提供了新思路。

Abstract: The motion planning problem requires finding a collision-free path between start and goal configurations in high-dimensional, cluttered spaces. Recent learning-based methods offer promising solutions, with self-supervised physics-informed approaches such as Neural Time Fields (NTFields) solving the Eikonal equation to learn value functions without expert demonstrations. However, existing physics-informed methods struggle to scale in complex, multi-room environments, where simply increasing the number of samples cannot resolve local minima or guarantee global consistency. We propose Hierarchical Neural Time Fields (H-NTFields), a weakly-supervised framework that combines weak supervision from sparse roadmaps with physics-informed PDE regularization. The roadmap provides global topological anchors through upper and lower bounds on travel times, while PDE losses enforce local geometric fidelity and obstacle-aware propagation. Experiments on 18 Gibson environments and real robotic platforms show that H-NTFields substantially improves robustness over prior physics-informed methods, while enabling fast amortized inference through a continuous value representation.

</details>


### [84] [GeoVision-Enabled Digital Twin for Hybrid Autonomous-Teleoperated Medical Responses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13248)
*Parham Kebria,Soheil Sabri,Laura J Brattain*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于数字孪生的混合自主-遥操作医疗响应系统架构，通过GeoVision能力实现远程医疗应急响应


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决灾害影响区和基础设施有限环境中远程医疗应急响应的挑战，传统地面控制界面缺乏直观性和实时性，需要增强远程临床和操作人员的态势感知与决策能力

Method: 提出数字孪生架构，集成感知与自适应导航，创建与物理系统实时同步的虚拟模型，映射系统状态、环境动态、患者状况和任务目标，提供直观的虚拟操作界面

Result: 数字孪生为远程用户提供持续更新的平台和操作环境虚拟表示，相比传统地面控制界面，显著增强态势感知能力，支持更明智的决策制定

Conclusion: 基于GeoVision的数字孪生架构为混合自主-遥操作医疗响应系统提供了创新的解决方案，通过实时同步的虚拟表示改善了远程医疗应急响应效果

Abstract: Remote medical response systems are increasingly being deployed to support emergency care in disaster-affected and infrastructure-limited environments. Enabled by GeoVision capabilities, this paper presents a Digital Twin architecture for hybrid autonomous-teleoperated medical response systems. The proposed framework integrates perception and adaptive navigation with a Digital Twin, synchronized in real-time, that mirrors system states, environmental dynamics, patient conditions, and mission objectives. Unlike traditional ground control interfaces, the Digital Twin provides remote clinical and operational users with an intuitive, continuously updated virtual representation of the platform and its operational context, enabling enhanced situational awareness and informed decision-making.

</details>


### [85] [Utilizing Inpainting for Keypoint Detection for Vision-Based Control of Robotic Manipulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13309)
*Sreejani Chatterjee,Venkatesh Mullur,Abhinav Gandhi,Berk Calli*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于自然视觉特征的机器人配置空间视觉伺服框架，通过数据驱动方法训练关键点检测算法，无需外部标记和精确相机标定，利用修复技术处理遮挡，结合UKF提升鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发无需外部标记的机器人视觉控制方法，适用于无法或不希望在机器人上放置标记的场景，消除对精确相机标定和机器人模型的依赖。

Method: 1) 数据收集管道：使用ArUco标记标注关键点，然后通过修复技术去除标记并重建遮挡区域，生成自然图像训练数据；2) 训练关键点检测算法；3) 运行时使用第二个修复模型实时重建机器人遮挡区域；4) 集成无迹卡尔曼滤波器(UKF)优化关键点估计。

Result: 成功实现了基于自然机器人特征的模型无关、纯视觉控制策略，在完全可见和部分遮挡条件下均获得成功控制结果。

Conclusion: 该框架通过修复技术和UKF实现了鲁棒的自然特征关键点检测，为无需外部标记的机器人视觉控制提供了有效解决方案，消除了对精确相机标定和机器人模型的依赖。

Abstract: In this paper we present a novel visual servoing framework to control a robotic manipulator in the configuration space by using purely natural visual features. Our goal is to develop methods that can robustly detect and track natural features or keypoints on robotic manipulators that would be used for vision-based control, especially for scenarios where placing external markers on the robot is not feasible or preferred at runtime. For the model training process of our data driven approach, we create a data collection pipeline where we attach ArUco markers along the robot's body, label their centers as keypoints, and then utilize an inpainting method to remove the markers and reconstruct the occluded regions. By doing so, we generate natural (markerless) robot images that are automatically labeled with the marker locations. These images are used to train a keypoint detection algorithm, which is used to control the robot configuration using natural features of the robot. Unlike the prior methods that rely on accurate camera calibration and robot models for labeling training images, our approach eliminates these dependencies through inpainting. To achieve robust keypoint detection even in the presence of occlusion, we introduce a second inpainting model, this time to utilize during runtime, that reconstructs occluded regions of the robot in real time, enabling continuous keypoint detection. To further enhance the consistency and robustness of keypoint predictions, we integrate an Unscented Kalman Filter (UKF) that refines the keypoint estimates over time, adding to stable and reliable control performance. We obtained successful control results with this model-free and purely vision-based control strategy, utilizing natural robot features in the runtime, both under full visibility and partial occlusion.

</details>


### [86] [Vectorizing Projection in Manifold-Constrained Motion Planning for Real-Time Whole-Body Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13323)
*Shrutheesh R Iyer,I-Chia Chang,Andrew Z. Liu,Yan Gu,Zachary Kingston*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于CPU SIMD加速的流形约束运动规划器，通过并行化投影式约束满足方法，实现了比现有方法快100-1000倍的速度提升，首次使实时约束运动规划成为可能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前流形约束运动规划方法对于复杂系统（如人形机器人）需要数十秒计算时间，在动态环境中不实用，需要加速以实现实时规划。

Method: 采用CPU SIMD并行加速技术，重新审视基于投影的约束满足方法，将相关组件转化为可并行化结构，利用SIMD并行性规划满足约束的解决方案。

Result: 相比现有最先进方法实现了100-1000倍的速度提升，首次使实时约束运动规划成为可能，并在真实人形机器人上展示了实时全身准静态规划生成能力。

Conclusion: 提出的SIMD加速流形约束运动规划器显著提升了计算效率，使复杂系统的实时约束运动规划变得可行，为动态环境中的机器人应用开辟了新可能性。

Abstract: Many robot planning tasks require satisfaction of one or more constraints throughout the entire trajectory. For geometric constraints, manifold-constrained motion planning algorithms are capable of planning collision-free path between start and goal configurations on the constraint submanifolds specified by task. Current state-of-the-art methods can take tens of seconds to solve these tasks for complex systems such as humanoid robots, making real-world use impractical, especially in dynamic settings. Inspired by recent advances in hardware accelerated motion planning, we present a CPU SIMD-accelerated manifold-constrained motion planner that revisits projection-based constraint satisfaction through the lens of parallelization. By transforming relevant components into parallelizable structures, we use SIMD parallelism to plan constraint satisfying solutions. Our approach achieves up to 100-1000x speed-ups over the state-of-the-art, making real-time constrained motion planning feasible for the first time. We demonstrate our planner on a real humanoid robot and show real-time whole-body quasi-static plan generation. Our work is available at https://commalab.org/papers/mcvamp/.

</details>


### [87] [Boundary Sampling to Learn Predictive Safety Filters via Pontryagin's Maximum Principle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13325)
*James Dallas,Thomas Lew,John Talbot,Jonathan DeCastro,Somil Bansal,John Subosits*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 使用庞特里亚金极大值原理生成边界轨迹指导数据收集，提高学习型汉密尔顿-雅可比可达性分析效率，实现高效安全滤波


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习型安全滤波器在高维系统中面临数据收集效率问题，需要包含可能导致约束违反的状态信息，但在复杂高维系统中难以有效采样

Method: 使用庞特里亚金极大值原理表征勉强避免安全违反的边界轨迹，以此指导数据收集，集中学习资源于安全关键状态附近，提高学习型汉密尔顿-雅可比可达性分析效率

Result: PMP采样显著提高学习效率，实现更快收敛、更低失败率、更优安全集重构，在共享控制汽车赛车应用中验证，计算时间约3毫秒

Conclusion: 基于PMP的边界轨迹采样为学习型可达性分析提供了高效的数据收集策略，能够显著改善安全滤波器的性能和学习效率

Abstract: Safety filters provide a practical approach for enforcing safety constraints in autonomous systems. While learning-based tools scale to high-dimensional systems, their performance depends on informative data that includes states likely to lead to constraint violation, which can be difficult to efficiently sample in complex, high-dimensional systems. In this work, we characterize trajectories that barely avoid safety violations using the Pontryagin Maximum Principle. These boundary trajectories are used to guide data collection for learned Hamilton-Jacobi Reachability, concentrating learning efforts near safety-critical states to improve efficiency. The learned Control Barrier Value Function is then used directly for safety filtering. Simulations and experimental validation on a shared-control automotive racing application demonstrate PMP sampling improves learning efficiency, yielding faster convergence, reduced failure rates, and improved safe set reconstruction, with wall times around 3ms.

</details>


### [88] [Singularity Avoidance in Inverse Kinematics: A Unified Treatment of Classical and Learning-based Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13405)
*Vishnu Rudrasamudram,Hariharasudan Malaichamee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文对机器人逆运动学中的奇异性问题进行了系统性综述，提出了统一的分类框架，并通过实验评估了12种IK求解器在Franka Panda机器人上的性能，发现纯学习方法效果不佳，而混合方法通过经典优化改进能显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 奇异性配置会导致任务空间移动性丧失、关节速度无界和求解器发散，现有研究缺乏连接经典奇异性鲁棒IK方法与快速发展的学习方法的综述。本文旨在填补这一空白，提供统一的理论框架和评估标准。

Method: 提出了一个系统性分类法，按保留的几何结构和鲁棒性保证（形式化vs经验性）对方法进行分类。建立了涵盖雅可比正则化、黎曼可操作性跟踪、约束优化和现代数据驱动范式的统一处理框架。设计了包含四个互补面板的基准测试协议：条件数误差退化、速度放大、分布外鲁棒性和计算成本。

Result: 实验评估了12种IK求解器在Franka Panda机器人上的位置IK性能。纯学习方法（如MLP）在良好条件目标上失败（0%成功率，约10mm平均误差），而混合预热启动架构（IKFlow 59%-100%、CycleIK 0%-98.6%、GGIK 0%-100%）通过经典细化显著提升性能。DLS能从高达207mm的初始误差收敛。

Conclusion: 纯学习方法在奇异性附近表现不佳，而混合方法结合了学习的泛化能力和经典优化的鲁棒性，能有效处理奇异性问题。需要进一步深入评估奇异性区域性能，这是未来的重要研究方向。

Abstract: Singular configurations cause loss of task-space mobility, unbounded joint velocities, and solver divergence in inverse kinematics (IK) for serial manipulators. No existing survey bridges classical singularity-robust IK with rapidly growing learning-based approaches. We provide a unified treatment spanning Jacobian regularization, Riemannian manipulability tracking, constrained optimization, and modern data-driven paradigms. A systematic taxonomy classifies methods by retained geometric structure and robustness guarantees (formal vs. empirical). We address a critical evaluation gap by proposing a benchmarking protocol and presenting experimental results: 12 IK solvers are evaluated on the Franka Panda under position-only IK across four complementary panels measuring error degradation by condition number, velocity amplification, out-of-distribution robustness, and computational cost. Results show that pure learning methods fail even on well-conditioned targets (MLP: 0% success, approx. 10 mm mean error), while hybrid warm-start architectures - IKFlow (59% to 100%), CycleIK(0% to 98.6%), GGIK (0% to 100%) - rescue learned solvers via classical refinement, with DLS converging from initial errors up to 207 mm. Deeper singularity-regime evaluation is identified as immediate future work.

</details>


### [89] [Robust Energy-Aware Routing for Air-Ground Cooperative Multi-UAV Delivery in Wind-Uncertain Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13441)
*Tianshun Li,Hongliang Lu,Yanggang Sheng,Zhongzhen Wang,Haoang Li,Xinhu Zheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向风敏感卡车辅助无人机配送的在线风险敏感规划框架BER，通过时间依赖的能量图路由和实时能量预算，在动态风环境下显著提高了任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在现实的卡车-无人机物流系统中，无人机必须在部分可观测的时变风条件下完成包裹配送并安全返回，而现有路由方法大多假设静态或确定性能量模型，在动态风环境中不可靠，需要确保风不确定性下的能量可行性以保证任务安全可靠。

Method: 提出电池高效路由(BER)框架，将问题建模为时间依赖能量图上的路由问题，边成本根据风致空气动力学效应演化。BER持续评估返回可行性，平衡瞬时能量消耗和不确定性感知风险，并嵌入到结合任务分配、路由和分散轨迹执行的分层空地配送架构中。

Result: 在Unreal Engine环境中生成的合成ER图和准真实风日志上的广泛仿真表明，BER相比静态和贪婪基线方法显著提高了任务成功率，减少了风致故障。

Conclusion: 结果强调了集成实时能量预算和环境感知对于动态风条件下无人机配送规划的重要性，BER框架为风敏感卡车辅助无人机配送提供了有效的在线风险敏感规划解决方案。

Abstract: Ensuring energy feasibility under wind uncertainty is critical for the safety and reliability of UAV delivery missions. In realistic truck-drone logistics systems, UAVs must deliver parcels and safely return under time-varying wind conditions that are only partially observable during flight. However, most existing routing approaches assume static or deterministic energy models, making them unreliable in dynamic wind environments. We propose Battery-Efficient Routing (BER), an online risk-sensitive planning framework for wind-sensitive truck-assisted UAV delivery. The problem is formulated as routing on a time dependent energy graph whose edge costs evolve according to wind-induced aerodynamic effects. BER continuously evaluates return feasibility while balancing instantaneous energy expenditure and uncertainty-aware risk. The approach is embedded in a hierarchical aerial-ground delivery architecture that combines task allocation, routing, and decentralized trajectory execution. Extensive simulations on synthetic ER graphs generated in Unreal Engine environments and quasi-real wind logs demonstrate that BER significantly improves mission success rates and reduces wind-induced failures compared with static and greedy baselines. These results highlight the importance of integrating real-time energy budgeting and environmental awareness for UAV delivery planning under dynamic wind conditions.

</details>


### [90] [RobotPan: A 360$^\circ$ Surround-View Robotic Vision System for Embodied Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13476)
*Jiahao Ma,Qiang Zhang,Peiran Liu,Zeran Su,Pihai Sun,Gang Han,Wen Zhao,Wei Cui,Zhang Zhang,Zhiyuan Xu,Renjing Xu,Jian Tang,Miaomiao Liu,Yijie Guo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出RobotPan系统，使用六摄像头+LiDAR实现360°环绕视觉，通过前馈框架预测度量尺度紧凑3D高斯用于实时渲染、重建和流式传输


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前机器人视觉接口局限于窄前向视野或多摄像头手动切换，存在运动抖动导致VR晕动症，需要满足几何和实时约束的360°视觉覆盖系统

Method: RobotPan框架将多视图特征提升到统一球坐标系表示，使用分层球形体素先验解码高斯，近机器人处精细分辨率、远处粗分辨率以减少计算冗余；在线融合更新动态内容，选择性更新静态区域外观防止无限增长

Result: 在保持竞争力的重建和视图合成质量的同时，产生显著更少的高斯，实现实用的实时部署；发布了专门用于360°新视图合成和度量3D重建的多传感器数据集

Conclusion: RobotPan系统为机器人导航和操作提供了完整的360°视觉覆盖，通过紧凑的3D高斯表示和分层处理实现了实时性能，推动了机器人视觉接口的发展

Abstract: Surround-view perception is increasingly important for robotic navigation and loco-manipulation, especially in human-in-the-loop settings such as teleoperation, data collection, and emergency takeover. However, current robotic visual interfaces are often limited to narrow forward-facing views, or, when multiple on-board cameras are available, require cumbersome manual switching that interrupts the operator's workflow. Both configurations suffer from motion-induced jitter that causes simulator sickness in head-mounted displays. We introduce a surround-view robotic vision system that combines six cameras with LiDAR to provide full 360$^\circ$ visual coverage, while meeting the geometric and real-time constraints of embodied deployment. We further present \textsc{RobotPan}, a feed-forward framework that predicts \emph{metric-scaled} and \emph{compact} 3D Gaussians from calibrated sparse-view inputs for real-time rendering, reconstruction, and streaming. \textsc{RobotPan} lifts multi-view features into a unified spherical coordinate representation and decodes Gaussians using hierarchical spherical voxel priors, allocating fine resolution near the robot and coarser resolution at larger radii to reduce computational redundancy without sacrificing fidelity. To support long sequences, our online fusion updates dynamic content while preventing unbounded growth in static regions by selectively updating appearance. Finally, we release a multi-sensor dataset tailored to 360$^\circ$ novel view synthesis and metric 3D reconstruction for robotics, covering navigation, manipulation, and locomotion on real platforms. Experiments show that \textsc{RobotPan} achieves competitive quality against prior feed-forward reconstruction and view-synthesis methods while producing substantially fewer Gaussians, enabling practical real-time embodied deployment. Project website: https://robotpan.github.io/

</details>


### [91] [RadarSplat-RIO: Indoor Radar-Inertial Odometry with Gaussian Splatting-Based Radar Bundle Adjustment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13492)
*Pou-Chun Kung,Yuan Tian,Zhengqin Li,Yue Liu,Eric Whitmire,Wolf Kienzle,Hrvoje Benko*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 首个基于高斯泼溅的雷达束调整框架，利用全距离-方位-多普勒数据联合优化雷达位姿与场景几何，显著降低雷达SLAM的漂移误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 雷达SLAM虽然对恶劣天气和光照条件更具鲁棒性，但现有方法主要依赖帧间里程计，导致显著的漂移误差。虽然闭环检测可以纠正长期误差，但需要重访地点且依赖鲁棒的地点识别。相比之下，视觉里程计通常利用束调整在局部窗口内联合优化位姿和地图，但雷达领域的等效束调整方法尚未得到充分探索。

Method: 提出首个基于高斯泼溅的雷达束调整框架，利用高斯泼溅这一密集且可微的场景表示方法。该方法使用全距离-方位-多普勒数据联合优化雷达传感器位姿和场景几何，首次将多帧束调整的优势引入雷达SLAM。该方法与现有的雷达惯性里程计前端集成，共同构成完整的SLAM系统。

Result: 在多个室内场景中，该方法相比先前的雷达惯性里程计取得了显著改进：平均绝对平移误差减少90%，平均绝对旋转误差减少80%。当与现有雷达惯性里程计前端集成时，该方法显著降低了位姿漂移并提高了系统的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该研究首次实现了雷达领域的束调整框架，通过高斯泼溅表示和全雷达数据优化，显著改善了雷达SLAM的精度和鲁棒性，为恶劣环境下的可靠定位与建图提供了新解决方案。

Abstract: Radar is more resilient to adverse weather and lighting conditions than visual and Lidar simultaneous localization and mapping (SLAM). However, most radar SLAM pipelines still rely heavily on frame-to-frame odometry, which leads to substantial drift. While loop closure can correct long-term errors, it requires revisiting places and relies on robust place recognition. In contrast, visual odometry methods typically leverage bundle adjustment (BA) to jointly optimize poses and map within a local window. However, an equivalent BA formulation for radar has remained largely unexplored. We present the first radar BA framework enabled by Gaussian Splatting (GS), a dense and differentiable scene representation. Our method jointly optimizes radar sensor poses and scene geometry using full range-azimuth-Doppler data, bringing the benefits of multi-frame BA to radar for the first time. When integrated with an existing radar-inertial odometry frontend, our approach significantly reduces pose drift and improves robustness. Across multiple indoor scenes, our radar BA achieves substantial gains over the prior radar-inertial odometry, reducing average absolute translational and rotational errors by 90% and 80%, respectively.

</details>


### [92] [A transformable slender microrobot inspired by nematode parasites for interventional endovascular surgery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13513)
*Xin Yang,Dongliang Fan,Yunteng Ma,Yuxuan Liao,Diancheng Li,U Kei Cheang,Bo Peng,Hongqiang Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 受线虫启发的可变形细长磁控微机器人，用于心血管疾病介入治疗，具有高灵活性、通过复杂血管的能力和药物输送功能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心血管疾病每年导致约1790万人死亡，治疗面临血管空间狭窄、拓扑结构复杂和手术风险高的挑战。现有机器人技术难以兼顾多功能性和机动性，需要开发新型微创手术机器人。

Method: 受寄生在人体血管系统中的线虫启发，开发了可变形细长磁控微机器人。优化了制造工艺和几何结构，采用超薄聚合物线材上均匀分布磁性珠粒的设计，头部设有一个大珠粒，长径比超过100（直径小于200微米，长度大于20毫米）。

Result: 机器人表现出优异的灵活性（最大曲率0.904 mm⁻¹）和运动能力（最大速度125 mm/s），能通过半径0.84 mm的急转弯和三维空间分布的孔洞。成功演示了在介入手术中的潜在应用：导航通过狭窄血管模型、包裹运输比自身重95倍的药物并释放到目标位置，以及通过变形缠绕在动脉瘤模型中实现栓塞应用。机器人还展示了出色的可注射性，能通过直径1.2 mm的医用针头抽吸和注射。

Conclusion: 该线虫启发的可变形细长磁控微机器人为心血管疾病介入治疗提供了有前景的解决方案，兼具多功能性、高机动性和微创特性，在药物输送、栓塞治疗等方面具有重要应用潜力。

Abstract: Cardiovascular diseases account for around 17.9 million deaths per year globally, the treatment of which is challenging considering the confined space and complex topology of the vascular network and high risks during operations. Robots, although promising, still face the dilemma of possessing versatility or maneuverability after decades of development. Inspired by nematodes, the parasites living, feeding, and moving in the human body's vascular system, this work develops a transformable slender magnetic microrobot. Based on the experiments and analyses, we optimize the fabrication and geometry of the robot and finally create a slender prototype with an aspect ratio larger than 100 (smaller than 200 microns in diameter and longer than 20 mm in length), which possesses uniformly distributed magnetic beads on the body of an ultrathin polymer string and a big bead on the head. This prototype shows great flexibility (largest curvature 0.904 mm-1) and locomotion capability (the maximum speed: 125 mm/s). Moreover, the nematode-inspired robot can pass through sharp turns with a radius of 0.84 mm and holes distributed in three-dimensional (3D) space. We also display the potential application in interventional surgery of the microrobot by navigating it through a narrow blood vessel mold to wrap and transport a drug (95 times heavier than the robot) by deforming the robot's slender body and releasing the drug to the aim position finally. Moreover, the robot also demonstrates the possible applications in embolization by transforming and winding itself into an aneurysms phantom and exhibits its outstanding injectability by being successfully withdrawn and injected through a medical needle (diameter: 1.2 mm) of a syringe.

</details>


### [93] [Stability Principle Underlying Passive Dynamic Walking of Rimless Wheel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13530)
*Fumihiko Asano*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 重新分析无轮缘轮被动行走的稳定性原理，基于运动方程线性化研究稳定性与能量守恒定律的关系


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无轮缘轮作为最简单的被动动态行走模型，其仅由重力效应产生的被动步态总是渐近稳定且1周期的，这源于自动满足两个稳定性必要条件：冲击姿态约束和恢复机械能约束。虽然已有通过动能递推公式证明稳定性的方法，但对内在稳定性原理的研究仍有深入空间。

Method: 基于运动方程的线性化重新考虑支撑相稳定性，研究稳定性与能量守恒定律的数学关系，通过数学分析深入理解内在稳定性原理

Result: 通过数学分析提供了对无轮缘轮内在稳定性原理的更深入理解，揭示了稳定性与能量守恒定律之间的本质联系

Conclusion: 该研究深化了对无轮缘轮被动行走稳定性原理的理解，通过运动方程线性化和能量守恒分析，为这一简单模型的稳定性机制提供了更全面的理论解释

Abstract: Rimless wheels are known as the simplest model for passive dynamic walking. It is known that the passive gait generated only by gravity effect always becomes asymptotically stable and 1-period because a rimless wheel automatically achieves the two necessary conditions for guaranteeing the asymptotic stability; one is the constraint on impact posture and the other is the constraint on restored mechanical energy. The asymptotic stability is then easily shown by the recurrence formula of kinetic energy. There is room, however, for further research into the inherent stability principle. In this paper, we reconsider the stability of the stance phase based on the linearization of the equation of motion, and investigate the relation between the stability and energy conservation law. Through the mathematical analysis, we provide a greater understanding of the inherent stability principle.

</details>


### [94] [Evolvable Embodied Agent for Robotic Manipulation via Long Short-Term Reflection and Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13533)
*Jianzong Wang,Botao Zhao,Yayun He,Junqing Peng,Xulong Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: EEAgent框架利用视觉语言模型实现机器人自我进化，通过长短时反射优化机制动态调整提示，在VIMA-Bench任务中达到最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器人方法存在训练需求大、跨任务泛化困难、缺乏可解释性等局限。提示学习为机器人自我进化提供了新机会，但如何从任务成功和失败中提取有意义的洞察仍然具有挑战性。

Method: 提出可进化具身智能体（EEAgent）框架，利用大型视觉语言模型进行环境理解和策略规划。引入长短时反射优化（LSTRO）机制，基于过去经验和新学到的教训动态优化提示，实现持续自我进化。

Result: 在六个VIMA-Bench任务上的评估表明，该方法达到了新的最先进水平，在复杂场景中显著优于基线方法。

Conclusion: EEAgent框架通过结合视觉语言模型和反射优化机制，实现了机器人的持续自我进化，提高了任务成功率，为解决通用机器人适应性问题提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Achieving general-purpose robotics requires empowering robots to adapt and evolve based on their environment and feedback. Traditional methods face limitations such as extensive training requirements, difficulties in cross-task generalization, and lack of interpretability. Prompt learning offers new opportunities for self-evolving robots without extensive training, but simply reflecting on past experiences.However, extracting meaningful insights from task successes and failures remains a challenge. To this end, we propose the evolvable embodied agent (EEAgent) framework, which leverages large vision-language models (VLMs) for better environmental interpretation and policy planning. To enhance reflection on past experiences, we propose a long short-term reflective optimization (LSTRO) mechanism that dynamically refines prompts based on both past experiences and newly learned lessons, facilitating continuous self-evolution, thereby enhancing overall task success rates. Evaluations on six VIMA-Bench tasks reveal that our approach sets a new state-of-the-art, notably outperforming baselines in complex scenarios.

</details>


### [95] [A Mechanistic Analysis of Sim-and-Real Co-Training in Generative Robot Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13645)
*Yu Lei,Minghuan Liu,Abhiram Maddukuri,Zhenyu Jiang,Yuke Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文通过理论分析和实验研究揭示了sim-and-real协同训练的内在机制，识别出结构化表示对齐和重要性重加权两个关键效应，并提出了一种改进的协同训练方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管协同训练（结合有限域内真实数据和丰富的替代数据如仿真或跨具身机器人数据）在训练生成式机器人策略中广泛应用且经验上成功，但其有效性的机制（何时、为何有效）仍不清楚。作者旨在深入理解协同训练的内部工作原理，促进该方向的研究。

Method: 通过理论分析和实证研究探讨sim-and-real协同训练机制。首先在玩具模型上进行控制实验验证识别出的效应，然后在sim-and-sim和sim-and-real机器人操作任务上进行广泛实验。分析为现有协同训练技术提供统一解释，并基于此提出一种简单改进方法。

Result: 识别出协同训练的两个内在效应：1）结构化表示对齐（跨域表示对齐与域可辨别性之间的平衡，对下游性能起主要作用）；2）重要性重加权效应（源于动作权重的域依赖调制，起次要作用）。实验验证了这些效应，提出的方法在先前方法基础上取得一致改进。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了协同训练的内在工作机制，提供了对现有技术的统一解释，并启发了改进方法。更广泛地，旨在深入理解协同训练的内部运作，促进该方向的研究发展。

Abstract: Co-training, which combines limited in-domain real-world data with abundant surrogate data such as simulation or cross-embodiment robot data, is widely used for training generative robot policies. Despite its empirical success, the mechanisms that determine when and why co-training is effective remain poorly understood. We investigate the mechanism of sim-and-real co-training through theoretical analysis and empirical study, and identify two intrinsic effects governing performance. The first, \textbf{``structured representation alignment"}, reflects a balance between cross-domain representation alignment and domain discernibility, and plays a primary role in downstream performance. The second, the \textbf{``importance reweighting effect"}, arises from domain-dependent modulation of action weighting and operates at a secondary level. We validate these effects with controlled experiments on a toy model and extensive sim-and-sim and sim-and-real robot manipulation experiments. Our analysis offers a unified interpretation of recent co-training techniques and motivates a simple method that consistently improves upon prior approaches. More broadly, our aim is to examine the inner workings of co-training and to facilitate research in this direction.

</details>


### [96] [Vision-and-Language Navigation for UAVs: Progress, Challenges, and a Research Roadmap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13654)
*Hanxuan Chen,Jie Zheng,Siqi Yang,Tianle Zeng,Siwei Feng,Songsheng Cheng,Ruilong Ren,Hanzhong Guo,Shuai Yuan,Xiangyue Wang,Kangli Wang,Ji Pei*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 关于无人机视觉语言导航（UAV-VLN）的综合性综述，涵盖任务定义、技术演进、资源生态、挑战分析和未来研究方向


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机视觉语言导航是具身人工智能的关键挑战，需要让无人机理解高级人类指令并在复杂3D环境中执行长时程任务。本文旨在对该领域进行全面系统的综述，从任务定义到最新技术进展，分析当前挑战并提出未来研究方向。

Method: 建立方法论分类体系，追踪从早期模块化方法到深度学习，再到当前基于大基础模型（包括视觉语言模型、视觉语言动作模型）的技术演进。系统回顾了仿真器、数据集和评估指标等关键资源生态系统，并对阻碍实际部署的主要挑战进行批判性分析。

Result: 提出了一个结构化的技术分类框架，总结了当前技术发展脉络：从模块化方法→深度学习→基于大基础模型的智能体系统→生成世界模型与VLA架构的物理基础推理集成。识别了仿真到现实差距、动态户外环境下的鲁棒感知、语言模糊性推理、资源受限硬件部署等核心挑战。

Conclusion: 通过综合分析当前基准和局限性，提出了前瞻性研究路线图，指导未来研究关注多智能体集群协调、空地协同机器人等关键前沿方向，推动UAV-VLN向实际应用发展。

Abstract: Vision-and-Language Navigation for Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (UAV-VLN) represents a pivotal challenge in embodied artificial intelligence, focused on enabling UAVs to interpret high-level human commands and execute long-horizon tasks in complex 3D environments. This paper provides a comprehensive and structured survey of the field, from its formal task definition to the current state of the art. We establish a methodological taxonomy that charts the technological evolution from early modular and deep learning approaches to contemporary agentic systems driven by large foundation models, including Vision-Language Models (VLMs), Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models, and the emerging integration of generative world models with VLA architectures for physically-grounded reasoning. The survey systematically reviews the ecosystem of essential resources simulators, datasets, and evaluation metrics that facilitates standardized research. Furthermore, we conduct a critical analysis of the primary challenges impeding real-world deployment: the simulation-to-reality gap, robust perception in dynamic outdoor settings, reasoning with linguistic ambiguity, and the efficient deployment of large models on resource-constrained hardware. By synthesizing current benchmarks and limitations, this survey concludes by proposing a forward-looking research roadmap to guide future inquiry into key frontiers such as multi-agent swarm coordination and air-ground collaborative robotics.

</details>


### [97] [Empirical Prediction of Pedestrian Comfort in Mobile Robot Pedestrian Encounters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13677)
*Alireza Jafari,Hong-Son Nguyen,Yen-Chen Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究通过实验分析移动机器人与行人交互的运动学变量与主观舒适度的关系，开发了基于最小距离、最小投影碰撞时间及综合变量的舒适度预测器，为机器人路径规划提供舒适度量化指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前移动机器人在公共空间（如人行道）运行时，多数研究关注客观安全性（如碰撞避免），但缺乏对行人主观舒适度的考虑。量化舒适度是机器人理解并响应人类情感的主要挑战，这阻碍了机器人实现更社会化的行为。

Method: 采用一对一实验设计，让移动机器人与志愿者交互，收集行人报告的舒适度数据。通过统计分析运动学变量与主观舒适度的相关性，基于实证发现设计了三种舒适度估计器/预测器：基于最小距离、基于最小投影碰撞时间、以及综合所有研究变量的复合估计器。

Result: 统计分析显示大多数运动学变量与行人舒适度存在中度但显著的相关性。复合预测器在所有预测器中达到最高的预测率和分类性能，其比值比为3.67，意味着当它识别行人为舒适时，行人实际舒适而非不舒适的可能性几乎是4倍。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了将行人感受纳入机器人路径规划的舒适度量化方法，为实现更社会合规的移动机器人奠定了基础。复合预测器能够有效评估行人舒适度，有助于开发更人性化的机器人导航系统。

Abstract: Mobile robots joining public spaces like sidewalks must care for pedestrian comfort. Many studies consider pedestrians' objective safety, for example, by developing collision avoidance algorithms, but not enough studies take the pedestrian's subjective safety or comfort into consideration. Quantifying comfort is a major challenge that hinders mobile robots from understanding and responding to human emotions. We empirically look into the relationship between the mobile robot-pedestrian interaction kinematics and subjective comfort. We perform one-on-one experimental trials, each involving a mobile robot and a volunteer. Statistical analysis of pedestrians' reported comfort versus the kinematic variables shows moderate but significant correlations for most variables. Based on these empirical findings, we design three comfort estimators/predictors derived from the minimum distance, the minimum projected time-to-collision, and a composite estimator. The composite estimator employs all studied kinematic variables and reaches the highest prediction rate and classifying performance among the predictors. The composite predictor has an odds ratio of 3.67. In simple terms, when it identifies a pedestrian as comfortable, it is almost 4 times more likely that the pedestrian is comfortable rather than uncomfortable. The study provides a comfort quantifier for incorporating pedestrian feelings into path planners for more socially compliant robots.

</details>


### [98] [Failure Identification in Imitation Learning Via Statistical and Semantic Filtering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13788)
*Quentin Rolland,Fabrice Mayran de Chamisso,Jean-Baptiste Mouret*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FIDeL是一个用于机器人模仿学习的独立故障检测模块，通过异常检测、最优传输匹配、保形预测和视觉语言模型语义过滤，能区分良性异常和真实故障，在BotFails数据集上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人模仿学习策略在受控环境中表现良好，但在实际部署中脆弱，无法处理训练分布外的罕见事件（硬件故障、意外人为动作等）。现有视觉异常检测方法能检测异常状态，但无法区分故障和良性偏差。

Method: FIDeL是一个策略独立的故障检测模块：1) 利用现有异常检测方法构建正常演示的紧凑表示；2) 通过最优传输匹配对齐输入观测生成异常分数和热图；3) 扩展保形预测推导时空阈值；4) 使用视觉语言模型进行语义过滤以区分良性异常和真实故障。

Result: 在BotFails（机器人故障检测多模态数据集）上，FIDeL持续优于最先进基线：异常检测AUROC提升5.30%，故障检测准确率提升17.38%。

Conclusion: FIDeL通过结合异常检测、最优传输、保形预测和视觉语言模型，有效解决了模仿学习中区分真实故障和良性异常的关键问题，显著提升了机器人系统的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Imitation learning (IL) policies in robotics deliver strong performance in controlled settings but remain brittle in real-world deployments: rare events such as hardware faults, defective parts, unexpected human actions, or any state that lies outside the training distribution can lead to failed executions. Vision-based Anomaly Detection (AD) methods emerged as an appropriate solution to detect these anomalous failure states but do not distinguish failures from benign deviations. We introduce FIDeL (Failure Identification in Demonstration Learning), a policy-independent failure detection module. Leveraging recent AD methods, FIDeL builds a compact representation of nominal demonstrations and aligns incoming observations via optimal transport matching to produce anomaly scores and heatmaps. Spatio-temporal thresholds are derived with an extension of conformal prediction, and a Vision-Language Model (VLM) performs semantic filtering to discriminate benign anomalies from genuine failures. We also introduce BotFails, a multimodal dataset of real-world tasks for failure detection in robotics. FIDeL consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, yielding +5.30% percent AUROC in anomaly detection and +17.38% percent failure-detection accuracy on BotFails compared to existing methods.

</details>


### [99] [Mosaic: An Extensible Framework for Composing Rule-Based and Learned Motion Planners](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13853)
*Nick Le Large,Marlon Steiner,Lingguang Wang,Willi Poh,Jan-Hendrik Pauls,Ömer Şahin Taş,Christoph Stiller*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Mosaic是一个可扩展的自动驾驶运动规划框架，通过仲裁图整合基于规则和学习的规划器，实现透明决策和冗余安全验证，在nuPlan基准测试中达到最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶运动规划面临安全性和可解释性的挑战：基于规则的规划器可预测但难以处理复杂交通场景，而学习型规划器适应性强但缺乏透明性且存在安全隐患。需要结合两者优势的解决方案。

Method: 提出Mosaic框架，使用仲裁图结构化决策机制，将轨迹验证和评分与轨迹生成解耦。通过高层轨迹验证在规划器间引入冗余，统一评分和最优轨迹选择，结合基于规则和学习型规划器的互补优势。

Result: 在nuPlan Val14闭环基准测试中达到95.48 CLS-NR和93.98 CLS-R，创下新记录，相比单独规划器减少30%责任碰撞。在interPlan交互场景基准测试中获得54.30 CLS-R，比最佳组成规划器提升23.3%，无需重新训练或额外数据。

Conclusion: Mosaic框架成功整合了基于规则和学习型规划器的优势，实现了透明、可追溯的决策过程，在保持安全性的同时提升了性能，为自动驾驶运动规划提供了可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Safe and explainable motion planning remains a central challenge in autonomous driving. While rule-based planners offer predictable and explainable behavior, they often fail to grasp the complexity and uncertainty of real-world traffic. Conversely, learned planners exhibit strong adaptability but suffer from reduced transparency and occasional safety violations. We introduce Mosaic, an extensible framework for structured decision-making that integrates both paradigms through arbitration graphs. By decoupling trajectory verification and scoring from the generation of trajectories by individual planners, every decision becomes transparent and traceable. Trajectory verification at a higher level introduces redundancy between the planners, limiting emergency braking to the rare case where all planners fail to produce a valid trajectory. Through unified scoring and optimal trajectory selection, rule-based and learned planners with complementary strengths and weaknesses can be combined to yield the best of both worlds. In experimental evaluation on nuPlan, Mosaic achieves 95.48 CLS-NR and 93.98 CLS-R on the Val14 closed-loop benchmark, setting a new state of the art, while reducing at-fault collisions by 30% compared to either planner in isolation. On the interPlan benchmark, focused on highly interactive and difficult scenarios, Mosaic scores 54.30 CLS-R, outperforming its best constituent planner by 23.3% - all without retraining or requiring additional data. The code is available at github.com/KIT-MRT/mosaic.

</details>


### [100] [Beyond Conservative Automated Driving in Multi-Agent Scenarios via Coupled Model Predictive Control and Deep Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13891)
*Saeed Rahmani,Gözde Körpe,Zhenlin,Xu,Bruno Brito,Simeon Craig Calvert,Bart van Arem*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出MPC-RL集成框架解决无信号交叉口自动驾驶问题，结合MPC的结构化约束处理与RL的自适应学习能力，在安全性和效率间取得更好平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无信号交叉口自动驾驶面临复杂的多车交互挑战，需要在安全与效率间取得平衡。传统MPC依赖人工规则导致过于保守，而纯RL方法难以保证安全性和泛化能力。

Method: 提出MPC-RL集成框架，结合模型预测控制的结构化优化约束处理与深度强化学习的自适应行为学习能力，通过MPC提供安全基础，RL优化决策策略。

Result: 实验显示MPC-RL在三种交通密度下均优于单独MPC和端到端RL：碰撞率降低21%，成功率提高6.5%（相比纯MPC）；在高速公路合流场景零样本迁移中，MPC方法比PPO泛化能力更强；训练过程中损失收敛更快。

Conclusion: 集成MPC-RL框架能改善多智能体交叉口场景的安全性能与效率平衡，MPC组件为跨驾驶环境的泛化提供了坚实基础，代码已开源。

Abstract: Automated driving at unsignalized intersections is challenging due to complex multi-vehicle interactions and the need to balance safety and efficiency. Model Predictive Control (MPC) offers structured constraint handling through optimization but relies on hand-crafted rules that often produce overly conservative behavior. Deep Reinforcement Learning (RL) learns adaptive behaviors from experience but often struggles with safety assurance and generalization to unseen environments. In this study, we present an integrated MPC-RL framework to improve navigation performance in multi-agent scenarios. Experiments show that MPC-RL outperforms standalone MPC and end-to-end RL across three traffic-density levels. Collectively, MPC-RL reduces the collision rate by 21% and improves the success rate by 6.5% compared to pure MPC. We further evaluate zero-shot transfer to a highway merging scenario without retraining. Both MPC-based methods transfer substantially better than end-to-end PPO, which highlights the role of the MPC backbone in cross-scenario robustness. The framework also shows faster loss stabilization than end-to-end RL during training, which indicates a reduced learning burden. These results suggest that the integrated approach can improve the balance between safety performance and efficiency in multi-agent intersection scenarios, while the MPC component provides a strong foundation for generalization across driving environments. The implementation code is available open-source.

</details>


### [101] [Towards Multi-Object-Tracking with Radar on a Fast Moving Vehicle: On the Potential of Processing Radar in the Frequency Domain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14013)
*Tim Hansen,Arturo Gomez-Chavez,Ilya Shimchik,Andreas Birk*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出在频域处理雷达数据以提高对噪声和结构误差的鲁棒性，特别针对高动态场景（如自动驾驶赛车超车），并展示了基于傅里叶变换的雷达里程计方法FS2D在Boreas数据集上的初步结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于特征的雷达处理方法在噪声和结构误差下鲁棒性不足，特别是在高动态场景中（车辆自身运动加上未知数量的其他运动物体）。频域处理不仅能提高鲁棒性，还能提供场景中所有运动结构的信息，这对于自动驾驶应用（如超车）至关重要。

Method: 采用频域处理雷达数据，使用基于相关性的方法（如配准）。具体提出了Fourier SOFT in 2D (FS2D)方法，在频域进行雷达数据处理，利用相关技术实现雷达里程计，无需传感器融合。

Result: 使用Boreas数据集进行了初步实验，展示了FS2D方法能够实现纯雷达里程计（radar-only-odometry），验证了频域处理在高动态场景中的有效性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 频域处理雷达数据相比传统特征方法具有更高的鲁棒性，特别适用于高动态场景，并能提供完整的运动结构信息。FS2D方法展示了纯雷达里程计的可行性，为自动驾驶应用（如赛车超车）提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: We promote in this paper the processing of radar data in the frequency domain to achieve higher robustness against noise and structural errors, especially in comparison to feature-based methods. This holds also for high dynamics in the scene, i.e., ego-motion of the vehicle with the sensor plus the presence of an unknown number of other moving objects. In addition to the high robustness, the processing in the frequency domain has the so far neglected advantage that the underlying correlation based methods used for, e.g., registration, provide information about all moving structures in the scene. A typical automotive application case is overtaking maneuvers, which in the context of autonomous racing are used here as a motivating example. Initial experiments and results with Fourier SOFT in 2D (FS2D) are presented that use the Boreas dataset to demonstrate radar-only-odometry, i.e., radar-odometry without sensor-fusion, to support our arguments.

</details>


### [102] [Scale-Invariant Sampling in Multi-Arm Bandit Motion Planning for Object Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14026)
*Servet B. Bayraktar,Andreas Orthey,Marc Toussaint*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种尺度不变采样策略，通过增长-收缩搜索寻找高熵采样尺度，结合PCA确定有效提取方向，显著提升狭窄空间物体提取的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决拆卸任务中物体提取的挑战：螺栓、螺钉、销钉等需要从毫米级狭窄空间移除。基于采样的规划器虽然能提供完备性保证，但几乎所有运动都会与环境碰撞，采样成为瓶颈。

Method: 提出尺度不变采样策略：1) 使用增长-收缩搜索探索配置空间，寻找有用且高熵的采样尺度；2) 找到有效尺度后，利用主成分分析(PCA)确定物体提取的有用方向；3) 将采样器嵌入多臂老虎机快速扩展随机树(MAB-RRT)规划器中。

Result: 在8个具有挑战性的3D物体提取场景（螺栓、齿轮、杆、销钉、插座）上进行测试。与经典采样策略（均匀采样、基于障碍物采样、窄通道采样）和现代策略（配对向量、基于物理规划、拆卸广度优先搜索）相比，尺度不变采样在7/8场景中将成功率提高了一个数量级。

Conclusion: 尺度不变采样是拆卸任务中通用物体提取的重要概念，能显著提升狭窄空间物体提取的成功率，解决了传统采样方法在毫米级间隙环境中的瓶颈问题。

Abstract: Object extraction tasks often occur in disassembly problems, where bolts, screws, or pins have to be removed from tight, narrow spaces. In such problems, the distance to the environment is often on the millimeter scale. Sampling-based planners can solve such problems and provide completeness guarantees. However, sampling becomes a bottleneck, since almost all motions will result in collisions with the environment. To overcome this problem, we propose a novel scale-invariant sampling strategy which explores the configuration space using a grow-shrink search to find useful, high-entropy sampling scales. Once a useful sampling scale has been found, our framework exploits this scale by using a principal components analysis (PCA) to find useful directions for object extraction. We embed this sampler into a multi-arm bandit rapidly-exploring random tree (MAB-RRT) planner and test it on eight challenging 3D object extraction scenarios, involving bolts, gears, rods, pins, and sockets. To evaluate our framework, we compare it with classical sampling strategies like uniform sampling, obstacle-based sampling, and narrow-passage sampling, and with modern strategies like mate vectors, physics-based planning, and disassembly breadth first search. Our experiments show that scale-invariant sampling improves success rate by one order of magnitude on 7 out of 8 scenarios. This demonstrates that scale-invariant sampling is an important concept for general purpose object extraction in disassembly tasks.

</details>


### [103] [UMI-3D: Extending Universal Manipulation Interface from Vision-Limited to 3D Spatial Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14089)
*Ziming Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: UMI-3D是通用操作接口(UMI)的多模态扩展，通过集成低成本LiDAR传感器解决原系统在遮挡、动态场景和跟踪失败等问题，提升数据收集的鲁棒性和可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原UMI系统依赖单目视觉SLAM，在遮挡、动态场景和跟踪失败时表现脆弱，限制了其在真实世界环境中的应用。需要开发更鲁棒的数据收集系统来支持大规模操作任务学习。

Method: 1) 在腕戴式接口中集成轻量级低成本LiDAR传感器；2) 开发LiDAR-centric SLAM实现精确度量尺度位姿估计；3) 构建硬件同步的多模态感知管道；4) 设计统一的时空标定框架对齐视觉观测与LiDAR点云；5) 保持原有的2D视觉运动策略公式。

Result: UMI-3D显著提高了收集数据的质量和可靠性，直接转化为策略性能提升。在标准操作任务上实现高成功率，并能学习原视觉系统难以处理的任务，包括大变形物体操作和关节物体操作。系统保持便携性和可访问性。

Conclusion: UMI-3D通过多模态感知解决了原UMI系统的局限性，为大规模数据收集和具身智能研究提供了鲁棒、可扩展的解决方案。所有硬件和软件组件已开源，促进研究社区发展。

Abstract: We present UMI-3D, a multimodal extension of the Universal Manipulation Interface (UMI) for robust and scalable data collection in embodied manipulation. While UMI enables portable, wrist-mounted data acquisition, its reliance on monocular visual SLAM makes it vulnerable to occlusions, dynamic scenes, and tracking failures, limiting its applicability in real-world environments. UMI-3D addresses these limitations by introducing a lightweight and low-cost LiDAR sensor tightly integrated into the wrist-mounted interface, enabling LiDAR-centric SLAM with accurate metric-scale pose estimation under challenging conditions. We further develop a hardware-synchronized multimodal sensing pipeline and a unified spatiotemporal calibration framework that aligns visual observations with LiDAR point clouds, producing consistent 3D representations of demonstrations. Despite maintaining the original 2D visuomotor policy formulation, UMI-3D significantly improves the quality and reliability of collected data, which directly translates into enhanced policy performance. Extensive real-world experiments demonstrate that UMI-3D not only achieves high success rates on standard manipulation tasks, but also enables learning of tasks that are challenging or infeasible for the original vision-only UMI setup, including large deformable object manipulation and articulated object operation. The system supports an end-to-end pipeline for data acquisition, alignment, training, and deployment, while preserving the portability and accessibility of the original UMI. All hardware and software components are open-sourced to facilitate large-scale data collection and accelerate research in embodied intelligence: \href{https://umi-3d.github.io}{https://umi-3d.github.io}.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [104] [Exploration and Exploitation Errors Are Measurable for Language Model Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13151)
*Jaden Park,Jungtaek Kim,Jongwon Jeong,Robert D. Nowak,Kangwook Lee,Yong Jae Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一个用于评估语言模型智能体探索与利用能力的可控环境框架，通过部分可观测的2D网格地图和未知任务DAG来量化智能体的探索与利用错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型智能体在复杂开放式决策任务中的应用日益广泛，但缺乏无需访问智能体内部策略就能系统区分和量化探索与利用行为的方法，这限制了对其决策能力的评估。

Method: 设计了受实际具身AI场景启发的可控环境，包含部分可观测的2D网格地图和未知任务DAG；通过程序化调整地图生成来强调探索或利用难度；设计了基于智能体动作的探索与利用错误量化指标。

Result: 评估了多种前沿语言模型智能体，发现即使是当前最先进的模型在该任务上也表现不佳，不同模型展现出不同的失败模式；推理模型能更有效地解决任务，且通过最小化工程改进可以显著提升探索与利用能力。

Conclusion: 该研究为语言模型智能体的探索与利用能力提供了可量化的评估框架，揭示了当前模型的局限性，并展示了通过工程改进提升这些能力的潜力，相关代码已开源。

Abstract: Language Model (LM) agents are increasingly used in complex open-ended decision-making tasks, from AI coding to physical AI. A core requirement in these settings is the ability to both explore the problem space and exploit acquired knowledge effectively. However, systematically distinguishing and quantifying exploration and exploitation from observed actions without access to the agent's internal policy remains challenging. To address this, we design controllable environments inspired by practical embodied AI scenarios. Each environment consists of a partially observable 2D grid map and an unknown task Directed Acyclic Graph (DAG). The map generation can be programmatically adjusted to emphasize exploration or exploitation difficulty. To enable policy-agnostic evaluation, we design a metric to quantify exploration and exploitation errors from agent's actions. We evaluate a variety of frontier LM agents and find that even state-of-the-art models struggle on our task, with different models exhibiting distinct failure modes. We further observe that reasoning models solve the task more effectively and show both exploration and exploitation can be significantly improved through minimal harness engineering. We release our code \href{https://github.com/jjj-madison/measurable-explore-exploit}{here}.

</details>


### [105] [SciFi: A Safe, Lightweight, User-Friendly, and Fully Autonomous Agentic AI Workflow for Scientific Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13180)
*Qibin Liu,Julia Gonski*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一个安全、轻量、用户友好的智能体框架，用于自主执行定义明确的科学任务，通过隔离环境、三层智能体循环和自评估机制确保可靠运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前智能体AI系统在现实世界科学研究中的可靠部署仍面临重大挑战，需要开发能够安全自主执行科学任务的框架，让研究人员能够将常规工作负载转移给AI，专注于创造性活动。

Method: 框架包含三个核心组件：1) 隔离执行环境确保安全；2) 三层智能体循环结构；3) 自评估do-until机制。专注于具有明确定义上下文和停止条件的结构化任务，支持端到端自动化。

Result: 该框架能够在不同能力水平的大型语言模型上实现安全可靠的运行，支持最小化人工干预的端到端自动化，有效减轻研究人员的常规工作负担。

Conclusion: 提出的智能体框架为科学研究中的自主任务执行提供了安全、轻量、用户友好的解决方案，使研究人员能够将更多精力投入到创造性活动和开放式科学探索中。

Abstract: Recent advances in agentic AI have enabled increasingly autonomous workflows, but existing systems still face substantial challenges in achieving reliable deployment in real-world scientific research. In this work, we present a safe, lightweight, and user-friendly agentic framework for the autonomous execution of well-defined scientific tasks. The framework combines an isolated execution environment, a three-layer agent loop, and a self-assessing do-until mechanism to ensure safe and reliable operation while effectively leveraging large language models of varying capability levels. By focusing on structured tasks with clearly defined context and stopping criteria, the framework supports end-to-end automation with minimal human intervention, enabling researchers to offload routine workloads and devote more effort to creative activities and open-ended scientific inquiry.

</details>


### [106] [ReSS: Learning Reasoning Models for Tabular Data Prediction via Symbolic Scaffold](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13392)
*Chenlang Yi,Gang Li,Zizhan Xiong,Tue Minh Cao,Yanmin Gong,My T. Thai,Tianbao Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ReSS框架通过结合符号决策树和LLM，生成高质量、忠实于决策逻辑的自然语言推理，用于微调专门的表格推理模型，在医疗和金融基准上提升性能10%


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在医疗和金融等高风险领域，表格数据预测模型需要高准确性和可解释的推理。符号模型可验证但表达能力有限，通用LLM需要专门微调才能掌握领域特定的表格推理。需要解决数据扩展和推理一致性的双重挑战。

Method: 提出ReSS框架：1) 使用决策树模型提取实例级决策路径作为符号支架；2) 用这些支架、输入特征和标签指导LLM生成严格遵循底层决策逻辑的自然语言推理；3) 用生成的高质量数据集微调预训练LLM为专门的表格推理模型；4) 采用支架不变的数据增强策略提升泛化能力和可解释性。

Result: 在医疗和金融基准测试中，ReSS训练的模型比传统决策树和标准微调方法提升高达10%，同时产生忠实且一致的推理。引入了幻觉率、解释必要性和解释充分性等定量指标来严格评估忠实性。

Conclusion: ReSS框架成功桥接了符号和神经推理模型，解决了表格数据推理中的可扩展数据生成和推理一致性问题，在保持高准确性的同时提供忠实、人类可理解的推理，适用于高风险领域。

Abstract: Tabular data remains prevalent in high-stakes domains such as healthcare and finance, where predictive models are expected to provide both high accuracy and faithful, human-understandable reasoning. While symbolic models offer verifiable logic, they lack semantic expressiveness. Meanwhile, general-purpose LLMs often require specialized fine-tuning to master domain-specific tabular reasoning. To address the dual challenges of scalable data curation and reasoning consistency, we propose ReSS, a systematic framework that bridges symbolic and neural reasoning models. ReSS leverages a decision-tree model to extract instance-level decision paths as symbolic scaffolds. These scaffolds, alongside input features and labels, guide an LLM to generate grounded natural-language reasoning that strictly adheres to the underlying decision logic. The resulting high-quality dataset is used to fine-tune a pretrained LLM into a specialized tabular reasoning model, further enhanced by a scaffold-invariant data augmentation strategy to improve generalization and explainability. To rigorously assess faithfulness, we introduce quantitative metrics including hallucination rate, explanation necessity, and explanation sufficiency. Experimental results on medical and financial benchmarks demonstrate that ReSS-trained models improve traditional decision trees and standard fine-tuning approaches up to $10\%$ while producing faithful and consistent reasoning

</details>


### [107] [Quantifying and Understanding Uncertainty in Large Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13395)
*Yangyi Li,Chenxu Zhao,Mengdi Huai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种为大型推理模型提供统计保证的推理-答案不确定性量化方法，以及基于Shapley值的可解释性框架


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法无法为推理-答案生成提供有限样本保证，现有方法忽略了推理轨迹与最终答案之间的逻辑联系，且缺乏对不确定性覆盖来源的可解释性分析

Method: 1) 提出量化推理-答案结构不确定性的新方法，具有统计保证；2) 开发基于Shapley值的统一示例-步骤解释框架，识别保持保证所需的训练示例子集和关键推理步骤

Result: 在具有挑战性的推理数据集上进行广泛实验，验证了所提方法的有效性

Conclusion: 该方法为大型推理模型提供了具有统计保证的不确定性量化，同时通过可解释性框架揭示了不确定性覆盖的来源，解决了推理质量与答案正确性分离的难题

Abstract: Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) have recently demonstrated significant improvements in complex reasoning. While quantifying generation uncertainty in LRMs is crucial, traditional methods are often insufficient because they do not provide finite-sample guarantees for reasoning-answer generation. Conformal prediction (CP) stands out as a distribution-free and model-agnostic methodology that constructs statistically rigorous uncertainty sets. However, existing CP methods ignore the logical connection between the reasoning trace and the final answer. Additionally, prior studies fail to interpret the origins of uncertainty coverage for LRMs as they typically overlook the specific training factors driving valid reasoning. Notably, it is challenging to disentangle reasoning quality from answer correctness when quantifying uncertainty, while simultaneously establishing theoretical guarantees for computationally efficient explanation methods. To address these challenges, we first propose a novel methodology that quantifies uncertainty in the reasoning-answer structure with statistical guarantees. Subsequently, we develop a unified example-to-step explanation framework using Shapley values that identifies a provably sufficient subset of training examples and their key reasoning steps to preserve the guarantees. We also provide theoretical analyses of our proposed methods. Extensive experiments on challenging reasoning datasets verify the effectiveness of the proposed methods.

</details>


### [108] [Towards Scalable Lightweight GUI Agents via Multi-role Orchestration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13488)
*Ziwei Wang,Junjie Zheng,Leyang Yang,Sheng Zhou,Xiaoxuan Tang,Zhouhua Fang,Zhiwei Liu,Dajun Chen,Yong Li,Jiajun Bu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LAMO框架使轻量级多模态大语言模型具备GUI特定知识和任务可扩展性，通过角色导向数据合成和两阶段训练，支持单体执行和多智能体系统编排，实现GUI自动化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决轻量级GUI智能体在资源受限设备上部署成本高、容量有限、任务可扩展性差的问题，特别是在复杂实际场景中，端到端情景学习限制了多智能体系统适应能力，而训练多个技能专家成本高昂。

Method: 提出LAMO框架：1) 角色导向数据合成；2) 两阶段训练：监督微调（使用困惑度加权交叉熵优化进行知识蒸馏和视觉感知增强）和强化学习（角色导向协同探索）；3) 开发LAMO-3B任务可扩展原生GUI智能体，支持单体执行和MAS式编排。

Result: LAMO-3B作为即插即用策略执行器，可与高级规划器配对，持续受益于规划器进步，实现更高性能上限。广泛的静态和在线评估验证了设计的有效性。

Conclusion: LAMO框架在成本-可扩展性困境中实现了有效权衡，使轻量级MLLM能够参与现实GUI工作流程，通过多角色编排扩展其GUI自动化能力边界。

Abstract: Autonomous Graphical User Interface (GUI) agents powered by Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) enable digital automation on end-user devices. While scaling both parameters and data has yielded substantial gains, advanced methods still suffer from prohibitive deployment costs on resource-constrained devices. When facing complex in-the-wild scenarios, lightweight GUI agents are bottlenecked by limited capacity and poor task scalability under end-to-end episodic learning, impeding adaptation to multi-agent systems (MAS), while training multiple skill-specific experts remains costly. Can we strike an effective trade-off in this cost-scalability dilemma, enabling lightweight MLLMs to participate in realistic GUI workflows? To address these challenges, we propose the LAMO framework, which endows a lightweight MLLM with GUI-specific knowledge and task scalability, allowing multi-role orchestration to expand its capability boundary for GUI automation. LAMO combines role-oriented data synthesis with a two-stage training recipe: (i) supervised fine-tuning with Perplexity-Weighted Cross-Entropy optimization for knowledge distillation and visual perception enhancement, and (ii) reinforcement learning for role-oriented cooperative exploration. With LAMO, we develop a task-scalable native GUI agent, LAMO-3B, supporting monolithic execution and MAS-style orchestration. When paired with advanced planners as a plug-and-play policy executor, LAMO-3B can continuously benefit from planner advances, enabling a higher performance ceiling. Extensive static and online evaluations validate the effectiveness of our design.

</details>


### [109] [Weight Patching: Toward Source-Level Mechanistic Localization in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13694)
*Chenghao Sun,Chengsheng Zhang,Guanzheng Qin,Rui Dai,Xinmei Tian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Weight Patching方法，通过参数空间干预在相同架构的配对模型中进行源导向分析，揭示能力表达的内部机制层次结构


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有激活空间定位和因果追踪方法存在局限：激活空间重要的模块可能只是聚合或放大上游信号，而非在自身参数中编码目标能力。需要参数空间干预方法来区分信号聚合与能力编码

Method: 提出Weight Patching方法：在具有相同架构但目标能力表达强度不同的配对模型（基础模型和行为专业化模型）中，将专业化模型的选定模块权重替换到基础模型中，观察行为变化。在指令跟随任务中实例化该方法，并引入基于向量锚点的行为接口框架

Result: 分析揭示了从浅层候选源侧载体到聚合路由模块，再到下游执行电路的层次结构。恢复的组件分数可指导机制感知的模型合并，在评估的专家组合中改善选择性融合

Conclusion: Weight Patching填补了参数空间干预的空白，为机制可解释性提供了源导向分析方法，能够区分信号聚合与能力编码，揭示模型内部机制的层次结构，并支持机制感知的模型合并

Abstract: Mechanistic interpretability seeks to localize model behavior to the internal components that causally realize it. Prior work has advanced activation-space localization and causal tracing, but modules that appear important in activation space may merely aggregate or amplify upstream signals rather than encode the target capability in their own parameters. To address this gap, we propose Weight Patching, a parameter-space intervention method for source-oriented analysis in paired same-architecture models that differ in how strongly they express a target capability under the inputs of interest. Given a base model and a behavior-specialized counterpart, Weight Patching replaces selected module weights from the specialized model into the base model under a fixed input. We instantiate the method on instruction following and introduce a framework centered on a vector-anchor behavioral interface that provides a shared internal criterion for whether a task-relevant control state has been formed or recovered in open-ended generation. Under this framework, the analysis reveals a hierarchy from shallow candidate source-side carriers to aggregation and routing modules, and further to downstream execution circuits. The recovered component scores can also guide mechanism-aware model merging, improving selective fusion across the evaluated expert combinations and providing additional external validation.

</details>


### [110] [Rethinking AI Hardware: A Three-Layer Cognitive Architecture for Autonomous Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13757)
*Li Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Tri-Spirit架构将AI智能分解为规划、推理和执行三层，分别映射到不同计算硬件，通过异步消息总线协调，显著降低延迟和能耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI系统（云端AI、设备端推理、边缘-云端流水线）将规划、推理和执行视为单一过程，导致不必要的延迟、能耗和碎片化的行为连续性。需要一种新的架构来优化异构硬件上的智能结构。

Method: 提出Tri-Spirit三层认知框架：规划层（Super Layer）、推理层（Agent Layer）和执行层（Reflex Layer），每层映射到不同的计算基板。通过异步消息总线协调，包含参数化路由策略、习惯编译机制（将重复推理路径提升为零推理执行策略）、收敛内存模型和显式安全约束。

Result: 在2000个合成任务的模拟评估中，相比云端中心和仅边缘基线，Tri-Spirit将平均任务延迟降低75.6%，能耗降低71.1%，LLM调用减少30%，离线任务完成率达到77.6%。

Conclusion: 认知分解而非单纯模型扩展是AI硬件系统级效率的主要驱动力。Tri-Spirit架构通过将智能分解为规划、推理和执行三层，并在异构硬件上优化映射，显著提升了系统性能。

Abstract: The next generation of autonomous AI systems will be constrained not only by model capability, but by how intelligence is structured across heterogeneous hardware. Current paradigms -- cloud-centric AI, on-device inference, and edge-cloud pipelines -- treat planning, reasoning, and execution as a monolithic process, leading to unnecessary latency, energy consumption, and fragmented behavioral continuity. We introduce the Tri-Spirit Architecture, a three-layer cognitive framework that decomposes intelligence into planning (Super Layer), reasoning (Agent Layer), and execution (Reflex Layer), each mapped to distinct compute substrates and coordinated via an asynchronous message bus. We formalize the system with a parameterized routing policy, a habit-compilation mechanism that promotes repeated reasoning paths into zero-inference execution policies, a convergent memory model, and explicit safety constraints. We evaluate the architecture in a reproducible simulation of 2000 synthetic tasks against cloud-centric and edge-only baselines. Tri-Spirit reduces mean task latency by 75.6 percent and energy consumption by 71.1 percent, while decreasing LLM invocations by 30 percent and enabling 77.6 percent offline task completion. These results suggest that cognitive decomposition, rather than model scaling alone, is a primary driver of system-level efficiency in AI hardware.

</details>


### [111] [AlphaCNOT: Learning CNOT Minimization with Model-Based Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13812)
*Jacopo Cossio,Daniele Lizzio Bosco,Riccardo Romanello,Giuseppe Serra,Carla Piazza*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AlphaCNOT：基于蒙特卡洛树搜索的强化学习框架，用于CNOT门最小化，在线性可逆合成中比PMH基线减少32%门数


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子电路优化是量子计算的核心任务，因为当前NISQ设备受限于误差传播，而CNOT门作为Clifford+T通用集中的唯一双量子比特门，其最小化尤为重要。现有启发式算法（如PMH）和基于强化学习的方法在拓扑约束合成中仍有改进空间。

Method: 提出AlphaCNOT，一个基于蒙特卡洛树搜索的强化学习框架，将CNOT最小化问题建模为规划问题。该方法采用基于模型的方法，利用前瞻搜索评估未来轨迹，从而找到更高效的CNOT序列。

Result: 在线性可逆合成中，相比PMH基线实现了高达32%的CNOT门数减少；在约束版本中，在多种最多8量子比特的拓扑结构上，相比最先进的基于强化学习的解决方案实现了持续的门数减少。

Conclusion: 强化学习与基于搜索策略的结合可应用于不同的电路优化任务（如Clifford最小化），从而促进向"量子效用"时代的过渡。

Abstract: Quantum circuit optimization is a central task in Quantum Computing, as current Noisy Intermediate Scale Quantum devices suffer from error propagation that often scales with the number of operations. Among quantum operations, the CNOT gate is of fundamental importance, being the only 2-qubit gate in the universal Clifford+T set. The problem of CNOT gates minimization has been addressed by heuristic algorithms such as the well-known Patel-Markov-Hayes (PMH) for linear reversible synthesis (i.e., CNOT minimization with no topological constraints), and more recently by Reinforcement Learning (RL) based strategies in the more complex case of topology-aware synthesis, where each CNOT can act on a subset of all qubits pairs. In this work we introduce AlphaCNOT, a RL framework based on Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) that address effectively the CNOT minimization problem by modeling it as a planning problem. In contrast to other RL- based solution, our method is model-based, i.e. it can leverage lookahead search to evaluate future trajectories, thus finding more efficient sequences of CNOTs.
